Southeast Cannabis & Hemp Business Directory — AUDITED

Last audited: 24 October 2026

Scope: Virginia (VA), West Virginia (WV), North Carolina (NC), South Carolina (SC), Georgia (GA) and Florida (FL) — local and regional businesses serving the cannabis and hemp industry. This is an independent audit and corrected rewrite of the original Southeast directory. The auditor did not write the original. Every business below keeps a source link next to it. Unverified entries are kept but clearly flagged with a visible UNVERIFIED badge; do not use them for outreach without the confirmation step named in Part 1, section “Still unverified”.

How to read the verdicts: Confirmed = own-site facts matched. Corrected = a contact, tag, name, address, scale or category was changed by the audit. Unverified = kept, flagged — the cannabis/hemp connection, a contact, or another key fact could not be confirmed from the business’s own site or a reliable public source. Already listed = already in the audited Mid-Atlantic directory; not duplicated here. Removed = positively shown closed, dissolved, merged or not a business. See the Method and Limits box — it limits what every verdict means.

Method and Limits — read before using any verdict

Do not soften this. The following is reproduced from the consolidated audit (§h and the Method and Limits section), honestly and in full substance:

  • Registry sweep (top priority, not done by any batch): VA SCC, WV SOS, NC SOS, SC SOS, GA SOS Corporations Division, FL Sunbiz — entry by entry, for at least every Corrected/Confirmed entry that will be presented as "operating", and every Unverified entry that might be upgraded. Only this sweep can turn the two Sunbiz dissolutions into a general closure picture.
  • Regulator licence check (§d): FL OMMU (MMTCs, certified labs), VA CCA (pharmaceutical processors, dispensing facilities, labs), WV OMC (growers, processors, dispensaries, labs), GA Access to Medical Cannabis Commission (low-THC production licensees, dispensaries), Eastern Band of Cherokee Indians tribal operation. Move out anything found, with licence number + regulator source.
  • Live-browser re-open of every search-indexed / 429-affected row in §b (labelled "search-indexed", "bot-check", "direct open not completed", "fetch failed"), plus footer/social checks for Cat 11 (graceshydro.com, gardencityhydro.net, Fifth Season hemp-growers page) and Cat 12 (Bear Dance about page, Charmed Hemp site, Chronic Boom Delights site). Do not upgrade a row to Confirmed on search text alone.
  • Credential lookups: state bar directories, CPA boards, VA DPOR Board for Contractors, state security/insurance-producer lookups, state departments of agriculture / USDA hemp records — for every number the original or a leaf file publishes as-published (notably Cat 3: CRC CA 0778135, Admiral CA 0H99364, NextGuard NY BR-1969329, SRS FL BK3120739/SL3257920; Cat 5–6: AK Security FL B2600249 / GA PSC002300 / TX B10503001; Cat 7–10: All Points FL DEP facility ID). Mark each Confirmed / Corrected / Unverified with the lookup name; never publish a number on as-published evidence alone.
  • Merge the 11 Already-listed entries into the Mid-Atlantic audited rewrite (including NOVA Hemp's new contacts and Marcus & Millichap's Florida evidence) and confirm no duplicate survives in the Southeast rewrite.
  • Re-check the flagged third-party-sourced contacts before publication: Cat 5 New River Distilling (third-party profile), Cat 12 Leafly-sourced phones/addresses (Charmed Hemp, Bee Well Botanicals), Cat 11 BBB-sourced listings, Cat 3–4 Land.com/LoopNet listings.
  • Build the rewrite from the leaf files' Rewrite entries sections (Standard category, Local/Regional/National, cannabis/hemp/both tag, corrected contacts, states served, key people, evidence sentence, credentials status) — this consolidation deliberately does not duplicate those 189 rewrite blocks. Add "Last audited: 24 October 2026" at the top, keep Unverified entries clearly flagged, and keep a source link next to every business.

    • *Consolidation method: leaf audit tables were copied programmatically into §b (verdict labels normalised only as described under §b; checked URLs and corrections otherwise verbatim from the leaf files). Counts were recomputed from those tables and cross-checked against each leaf file's own verdict-counts section; the only arithmetic adjustment is Pee Dee Extractors, counted in Corrected and in §e (see C-4). No batch failed or was incomplete in the sense of missing entries — all 207 main entries and both candidates have a row — but every batch was incomplete in the registry/licence-lookup sense described in Method and limits, and that incompleteness is carried forward here rather than hidden.*

    In short, the four limits that govern this whole document:

    • No systematic entry-by-entry state-registry sweep was completed. The registries (Virginia State Corporation Commission; West Virginia, North Carolina, South Carolina and Georgia Secretaries of State; Florida Division of Corporations / Sunbiz) are form-gated/interactive and could not be run by text fetch. A “Confirmed” verdict therefore does not mean registry-verified, unless the row says so.
    • No systematic FL OMMU / VA CCA / WV OMC / GA Commission / tribal licence sweep was run, so “0 licensed-operator move-outs” is an absence of positive findings, NOT a clearance. No batch positively showed a state or tribal cannabis licence among the listed businesses; that does not prove that no listed business holds such a licence. The regulator check in §h must be run before publication.
    • Several official-site fetches hit 429 rate limits and were not retried, so those rows rest on search-indexed own-site text labelled as such. A 429/fetch failure is a tool failure, not evidence that a site is down, and search text alone does not earn a Confirmed verdict.
    • Credentials are overwhelmingly “Unverified — number not published / lookup not run” and NO licence number in this document is registry-Confirmed. Every number that appears is as-published by the business or a leaf file and must be re-checked against its lookup before publication. No licence number was invented.

    “Removed” is used only where a leaf auditor positively showed closure, dissolution, merger or non-business status (named below). It is not a registry finding except for the two Category 9 Sunbiz cases. Same-name traps were checked per batch (Greenville NC vs SC; Jacksonville FL vs NC; Charleston SC vs WV; Columbus, Athens and Augusta GA vs other states; Richmond VA vs KY; Washington NC vs DC; Bristol VA vs TN) and are flagged in individual rows where they bit.

    What changed vs the original — headline corrections

    • Mergers: Daszkal Bolton, LLP removed as a standalone firm — merged into CohnReznick effective 1 March 2023. No CohnReznick cannabis practice is asserted in its place.
    • Closures / dissolutions: HempStaff, LLC (FL Sunbiz administratively dissolved 22 September 2023, L14000047284; BBB “out of business”); Blaze Staffing & Recruiting, LLC (FL Sunbiz revoked 24 September 2021, M20000004122); Grow VA, Virginia Beach (Permanently Closed); District Hemp Botanicals (permanently closed per the Mid-Atlantic finding).
    • Non-entry removed: Category 4 “Brokerage not named / Russ Brown” (SC-01) — no brokerage could be positively identified on the Land.com listing (Pedersen Farm, Branchville, SC; listing last updated August 2022).
    • Tag corrections: CRC Group and HUB International (Winter Park) corrected from Both to cannabis-only on the pages checked — hemp insurance was not evidenced on those pages. Georgia CannaBusiness Chamber of Commerce corrected from Both to cannabis. Bowles Rice narrowed to medical cannabis on its own page. Longo & Alach narrowed from Both to cannabis. Green Check Verified corrected to cannabis (general serving cannabis). Several other tags were narrowed or widened on own-site evidence; each is recorded in the audit table.
    • Category moves (listed once, under original grouping, with a note): Bear Dance Hemp Company, Category 12 → Compliance and Consulting (own site: “We’re just not doing retail anymore” — now consultations, product guidance/education, B2B consulting); The Hemp Mine, Category 10 → Hemp and CBD Products (primary, finished-product seller in 2,000+ stores) with a Compliance and Consulting cross-reference; MORR, Category 5 → Grow Supplies and Equipment; New River Distilling Company, Category 5 → Extraction and Lab Equipment.
    • Contacts corrected or removed where not business-published: e.g. Womble Bond Dickinson Atlanta address corrected to 1331 Spring St NW, Suite 1400, Atlanta, GA 30309; William C. Head corrected to Sandy Springs (5600 Roswell Rd, Building H #210, Sandy Springs, GA 30342) plus Marietta, Alpharetta and Downtown Atlanta offices; Minardi Law address/phone/email resolved to the firm site (2534 W. Curtis St, Tampa, FL 33614; 813-995-8227); QuadScore address corrected to 980 Hammond Dr Suite 1400, Sandy Springs, GA 30328; Green Check Verified phone corrected to 1-844-544-1940 and its RocketReach Bonita Springs address removed; Safe Dispose Tampa base removed as secondary-only. “Not published” is used wherever the business does not publish a detail — it is not filled from directories.
    • De-duplication: 11 businesses already in the audited Mid-Atlantic directory are cross-referenced, not duplicated (see Already listed above), including Greenspoon Marder, Vicente, PSA, Marcus & Millichap, Pizzano Contractors, Brink’s/PAI, Empyreal Logistics, Clean Management, Hazardous Waste Experts, 5WPR and NOVA Hemp.
    • 280E flags resolved on own-site evidence: Cherry Bekaert and Garcia Tax and Accounting now expressly show 280E on their own pages; CRI’s brochure still does not quote 280E, so no 280E expertise is claimed for CRI.
    • Unplaced candidates stay out: Legler McCraw (cannabis practice confirmed, no Southeast office/contacts found) and DPS Armored (positively out-of-region, Greater Seattle/Washington State) are not added.
    • Licensed operators: none positively shown, so none moved out — but see the Method and Limits caveat: that is not a clearance, and the regulator sweep must still be run.

    Appendix — conflicts between batches and [Verify]-flag resolutions

    Reproduced from consolidated audit §§f–g, so the reader can see where batches disagreed and what could not be reconciled. These do not change the counts above.

  • C-1 — District Hemp Botanicals (Cat 12, VA-09): Removed vs Already listed. The Cat 12 auditor found the firm in the audited Mid-Atlantic directory, but that Mid-Atlantic verdict is itself Removed (permanently closed). Resolution: consolidated verdict Removed, and the firm is NOT counted in the 11 Already-listed entries. Rationale: "Already listed" is for entries that live on in the Mid-Atlantic rewrite; a closed firm does not live on anywhere. The parent must not re-add it as an active duplicate on either list.
  • C-2 — NOVA Hemp (Cat 12, VA-04): Already listed, with new contacts. Resolution: consolidated verdict Already listed; the new own-site contacts found in this audit (540-227-4119, Info@nova-cbd.com, Facebook/X/Instagram handles — full detail in the Cat 12 leaf file) are to be merged into the Mid-Atlantic entry. They do not create a Southeast entry and do not change the Mid-Atlantic entry's own verdict.
  • C-3 — Marcus & Millichap (Cat 4) and PSA (Cat 3): Already listed by parent/office, not by exact office string. Resolution: both counted Already listed. Marcus & Millichap's Southeast evidence (Fort Lauderdale team: Barry Wolfe / Alan Lipsky) merges into the single Mid-Atlantic entry (Philadelphia office in that audit); PSA's Richmond VA HQ / Maryland cannabis page is one firm, one entry. The parent must not list either firm twice with different offices as if they were different businesses.
  • C-4 — Pee Dee Extractors (Cat 12, SC-05): Corrected vs moved-out. The leaf table verdict is "Corrected (moved)". Resolution: counted in the Corrected tally (82) and simultaneously in the hemp-licence-only list (§e), and excluded from the 189-entry rewrite main list. It is not counted as Removed and not double-counted in the 207 arithmetic (see §a check).
  • C-5 — Bear Dance Hemp Company (Cat 12, VA-08): category move, not a removal. Its own site (search-indexed, flagged "open before publication" in the leaf file) says "We're just not doing retail anymore" — now consultations/education/B2B consulting. Resolution: verdict Corrected, Standard category Compliance and Consulting, counted once in the 189-entry rewrite main list under its new category, with corrected address 829 Salem Ave SW Suite B, Roanoke, VA 24016 and phone (540) 251-4361. It must not also appear in Cat 12.
  • C-6 — The Hemp Mine (Cat 10): category move, not a hemp-licence-only move. Batch D changed its primary category to Hemp and CBD Products (2,000+ retail stores, finished products sold) with a Compliance cross-reference, and explicitly found no hemp-licence-only move. Resolution: counted once, under the Cat 10 batch, verdict Corrected, Standard category Hemp and CBD Products. It must not be duplicated in Cat 12.
  • C-7 — Legler McCraw: Batch A scope vs Batch D decision. Batch A's brief excluded the Cat 2 candidate; Batch D audited it and kept it unplaced. Resolution: the candidate is handled only in §b's candidate table, with Batch D's decision. It is not in any batch's main counts and not in the 189.
  • C-8 — DPS Armored: Florida assignment rejected. The original held DPS Armored as a Cat 7 candidate (implying a possible Florida placement). Batch D positively established a Greater Seattle / Washington State service area. Resolution: keep unplaced, do not assign to Florida or any Southeast state.
  • C-9 — Blaze Staffing (Cat 9): original "Inactive" vs audit "Removed". The original counted Cat 9 as "5 (4 active, Blaze Inactive)". Batch D found FL Sunbiz revocation (24 Sep 2021, doc M20000004122). Resolution: consolidated verdict Removed; Cat 9's rewrite count is therefore 3 kept (1 Confirmed + 1 Corrected + 1 Unverified), not 4.
  • C-10 — HempStaff (Cat 9) vs Cat 9's other staffing firms. No conflict with another batch, but recorded here because two Cat 9 removals could be mistaken for a batch error: HempStaff (Sunbiz dissolved 22 Sep 2023, doc L14000047284) and Blaze (above) are distinct firms with distinct Sunbiz records. Both are Removed.
  • C-11 — Cat 11 Standard-category splits (AE Global, Vivid, TPC). The original Cat 11 mixes marketing agencies and grow stores. Batch E assigned AE Global to Packaging and Labelling (not Marketing), Vivid Printing Hub to Packaging and Labelling, and TPC Packaging Solutions to Packaging and Labelling (Unverified on cannabis connection). Resolution: the original-category counts in §a keep all 30 in Cat 11; the parent must use Batch E's Standard categories in the rewrite, not the original Cat 11 label.
  • C-12 — Cat 5 New River Distilling. Batch C re-categorised it to Extraction and Lab Equipment, with website/address/phone found via a third-party profile and flagged Verify. Resolution: counted in Cat 5's original-category totals, but the rewrite must use Extraction and Lab Equipment and must re-check the third-party-sourced contacts on the firm's own site before publication.
  • C-13 — No internal cross-batch duplicates found. Business names were compared across all six leaf tables (normalised name comparison) and no business appears in two batches as a separate entry, other than the Mid-Atlantic overlaps in §a and the candidate handling in C-7/C-8. Near-names that were checked and kept separate include: Charleston Hemp Collective (SC-02) vs Charleston Hemp Company (SC-03) vs Charleston, WV (WV-07 Green Infusions CBD); Blue Ridge Hydroponics (VA, Cat 11) vs Blue Ridge Hemp / Blue Ridge Extractions (NC, Cat 12) vs Blue Ridge Creative Marketing (NC, Cat 11); Greenacres Greenery (SC, Cat 12) vs GreenAcre (Cat 10, Unverified, directory-only) vs Green Dot Advisors (Cat 10); Cypress Hemp (VA, Cat 12) vs any Mid-Atlantic Cypress entry (none found); Jacksonville Beach, FL (Cat 12 FL-09) vs Jacksonville, NC; Groundworks (Cat 11, VA-08, Unverified) vs the foundation-repair firm of the same name (name trap, not a merge).
  • C-14 — Cat 10 "GA entry with no clear business name" (from the coordinator brief). No batch reported an unnamed Cat 10 entry; Batch D's Cat 10 table has 8 named entries (CommonLeafVA, Green Dot Advisors, ABC Consulting, cLoud-Share, The Hemp Mine, GACBCC, HempCertify, GreenAcre). Resolution: no unnamed entry to resolve; if the parent finds one in the original HTML row, it was not in the leaf extracts and needs a fresh check, not an assumed identity.
  • C-15 — 429 / search-indexed evidence vs "site opened" claims. Several batches report direct opens succeeded early, then hit a 429 rate limit, and later rows rest on search-indexed own-site text labelled as such. Resolution: this consolidation preserves each row's own wording ("opened directly" vs "search-indexed") verbatim in §b and does NOT upgrade search-indexed rows to "opened". The parent must treat every search-indexed row as needing a live-browser open before publication (see §h).

    • Limitation first. The original uses [Verify] at field level and inconsistently across categories (e.g. Cat 1 has 143 "Verify" occurrences but 0 literal "[Verify]" strings; Cat 11 has 218 literal "[Verify]" strings; Cat 9 has 0). The leaf notes do not carry a per-field [Verify]-resolution index, so a complete field-by-field reconciliation cannot be reconstructed from the leaf files without re-reading the original extracts field by field. The list below is therefore only the explicit resolutions and contradictions stated in the leaf files — it is not a claim that every original [Verify] was resolved, and the parent must not present it as one. Any original [Verify] not listed here remains open unless the §b row's corrections show it resolved.
